# Scylla

[Scylla]
Type: Weapon ([[Weapons]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Dungeon ([[Dungeons]]) Completion: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Stats: [[Damage]]: 270 (max 320); [[Strength]]: 150; [[Crit Chance]]: 12%; [[Crit Damage]]: 35% (max 85%); [[Ferocity]]: 30; [[Intelligence]]: 50
Gemstones: ⚔ Combat Gemstone Slot (Applicable Gemstones Ruby Amethyst Sapphire Jasper Onyx Opal; Cost to unlock 250,000 coins 1 Flawless Jasper 1 Flawless Sapphire 1 Flawless Ruby 1 Flawless Amethyst) ⚔ Combat Gemstone Slot (Applicable Gemstones Ruby Amethyst Sapphire Jasper Onyx Opal; Cost to unlock 250,000 coins 1 Flawless Jasper 1 Flawless Sapphire 1 Flawless Ruby 1 Flawless Amethyst)

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SCYLLA
Tooltip: Gear Score: 527 (574); Damage: +270 (+297); Strength: +150 (+165); Crit Chance: +12% (+12%); Crit Damage: +35% (+38.5%); Ferocity: +30 (+30); Intelligence: +50 (+55); Gemstones: [⚔] [⚔]; Deals +50% damage to  Wither mobs.; Grants +1  Damage and +1  Crit Damage per Catacombs level.; Right-click to use your class ability!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor VII Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON SWORD
The Scylla is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked upon completion of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
The Scylla is one of the four [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] variants, alongside the [[Hyperion]], [[Valkyrie]], and [[Astraea]], focusing on [[Crit Chance]], [[Crit Damage]] and [[Archer]] builds.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|8 [[Bigfoot's Bola]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Hyperion]] [[Astraea]] [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: [[Bigfoot's Bola]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Hyperion]]/[[Astraea]]/[[Valkyrie]] → Scylla|
## Usage
Sword:
Scylla [[Damage]]: +270 [[Strength]]: +150 [[Crit Chance]]: +12 [[Crit Damage]]: +35 [[Ferocity]]: +30 [[Intelligence]]: +50
[Effects]
[Deals +50% damage to Wither mobs.; Grants +1 Damage and +1 Crit Damage per Catacombs level.; Right-click to use your class ability!]
### Crafting
The Scylla can be crafted into another [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] variant using the corresponding recipe, keeping all enchantments and upgrades. Items used in the crafting recipe are consumed.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Valkyrie]]|None|8 [[Diamante's Handle]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] Scylla [[Hyperion]] [[Astraea]]|Recipe: [[Diamante's Handle]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/Scylla/[[Hyperion]]/[[Astraea]] → [[Valkyrie]]|
|[[Hyperion]]|None|8x [[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] Scylla [[Astraea]] [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: [[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/Scylla/[[Astraea]]/[[Valkyrie]] → [[Hyperion]]|
|[[Astraea]]|None|8 [[Jolly Pink Rock]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] Scylla [[Hyperion]] [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: [[Jolly Pink Rock]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/Scylla/[[Hyperion]]/[[Valkyrie]] → [[Astraea]]|
It can have [[Necron's Blade Scrolls]] applied to it, granting their effects on right click.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|150|150|
|✪✪|300|450|
|✪✪✪|500|950|
|✪✪✪✪|900; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,850; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|1,500; 25,000 [[Coins]]|3,350; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- Ironically, despite being geared towards [[Archer|Archers]], the Scylla is not practical in execution due to being a [[Swords|Sword]] and not a [[Bows|Bow]]. Additionally, Archer builds are not geared towards high [[Intelligence]], which is required to use Implosion, Wither Shield, and Shadow Warp efficiently.
  - The Scylla's [[Crit Damage]] previously made it useful for Wither Shield, which granted 150% of the player's [[Crit Damage]] as an [[Absorption]] shield. Wither Shield was changed to scale depending on [[Catacombs]] level, removing this functionality.
## Trivia
- The Scylla references the sea monster of Greek mythology, who devoured sailors in the Odyssey.
- The Scylla and [[Astraea]] are tied for granting the most unique stats, at six.
- Donating a Scylla to the Museum gives credit for the [[Astraea]] and [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]].

# Scythe Blade
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Zombie Slayer, Mob Drops
[Scythe Blade]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

Item ID: SCYTHE_BLADE
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; LEGENDARY
A Scythe Blade is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item. It is unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
A Scythe Blade is a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drop from the Tier IV [[Revenant Horror]] and Tier V [[Revenant Horror|Atoned Horror]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier IV: 0.06% (1/1761)
- Tier V: 0.1% (1/968)
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Reaper Scythe]]|Zombie Slayer VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|[[Scythe Blade]] 1x Scythe Blade 3x [[Reaper Falchion]] 64x [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: Scythe Blade + [[Revenant Viscera]] x64 + [[Reaper Falchion]] ×3 → [[Reaper Scythe]]|
|[[Reaper Orb]]|Zombie Slayer VIII (8) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|[[Scythe Blade]] 8x Scythe Blade|Recipe: Scythe Blade ×8 → [[Reaper Orb]]|
## Tips
- It is generally better to wait to obtain 8 Scythe Blades, as selling 1 [[Reaper Orb]] results in an additional profit of per Scythe Blade.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Added Scythe Blade.|
|Mar 12, 2021|Scythe Blade can now be used to craft the [[Reaper Orb]].|

The Seal Hoodie Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Seal Hoodie Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 7, 2026. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 2,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Seal Hoodie Skin can be applied to a [[Shark Scale Armor|Shark Scale Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 20 different versions.
- Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 20 hoodie variants.

Secret Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Secret Dye has a 0.0001% (1 in 1,000,000) chance to be obtained from [[Dungeon Secrets]]. Upon finding the dye, it will instantly be added to your inventory.
## Usage
Secret Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #7D7D7D. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

A Severed Hand is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrade]] that can be applied to any [[Weapons|Weapon]] with [[Smite|Smite VI]] to upgrade it to [[Smite|Smite VII]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
A Severed Hand has a 0.07% chance to drop from the Tier IV [[Revenant Horror]] or higher if the player is [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VII (7)]] or above, and can be guaranteed upon reaching 1,049,785 XP in [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]].
### Experimentation Table
A Severed Hand also has a 0.0263% chance to be obtained from [[Experiments|Superpairs]] and can be guaranteed upon reaching 500,000 XP in [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]].
### Legacy Trades
The player can trade in an 1 [[Enchanted Book]] ([[Smite|Smite VII]]) for a Severed Hand.
## Usage
A Severed Hand can be applied in an anvil to any weapon that has [[Smite|Smite VI]] in order to upgrade it to [[Smite|Smite VII]].
## History

The Shadow Fury is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Dungeon Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Shadow Fury can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor V]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|

## Usage
Sword:
Shadow Fury [[Damage]]: +300 [[Strength]]: +130 [[Crit Damage]]: +30 [[Speed]]: +30
[Effects]
[Ability: Shadow Fury RIGHT CLICK; Rapidly teleports you to up to 5 enemies within 12 blocks, rooting each of them and allowing you to hit them.; Cooldown: 15s]
Sword:
[[Shadow Fury]] [[Damage]]: +310 [[Strength]]: +160 [[Crit Damage]]: +60 [[Speed]]: +40
[Effects]
[Ability: Shadow Fury RIGHT CLICK; Rapidly teleports you to up to 5 enemies within 12 blocks, rooting each of them and allowing you to hit them.; Cooldown: 15s]
The Shadow Fury, being a sword, can be used as a melee weapon. Its Shadow Fury ability allows the wielder to teleport behind up to 5 enemies within a radius of 12 blocks in rapid succession, dealing damage to them based on your currently held item. This ability has a 15s cooldown and has no [[Mana]] cost.
## Upgrading
A Shadow Fury can be upgraded into Shadow Fury using 8 [[Livid Fragment|Livid Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +10 [[Damage]]
- +30 [[Strength]]
- +30 [[Crit Damage]]
- +10 [[Speed]]
- Grants an additional ⚔ Combat [[Gemstone Slot]]
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Shadow Fury]] 1x Shadow Fury 8x [[Livid Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Livid Fragment]] ×8 + Shadow Fury → [[Shadow Fury]]|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|50|50|
|✪✪|100|150|
|✪✪✪|200|350|
|✪✪✪✪|400; 10,000 [[Coins]]|750; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|800; 25,000 [[Coins]]|1,550; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The Shadow Fury is a reference to the Dark Templar's Shadow Fury ability in the campaign of StarCraft 2: Legacy of the Void, in which it teleports around a chosen area, striking up to 5 enemies, before returning to its original position. Vorazun in both the aforementioned campaign as well as the co-op missions gamemode also has this ability.
  - It is also potentially a reference to the Void Crawler's previous ultimate in Smash Heroes, in which he would teleport to random players up to 5 times, blinding them in the process whilst being able to attack them.
- The Shadow Fury bears a resemblance to the [[Silent Death]]: both allow the player to teleport behind enemies to attack them, and both are related to [[Shadow Assassin|Shadow Assassins]].

The Shattered Locket is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] material used in forging [[Pendant of Divan]].
## Obtaining
A Shattered Locket has a base chance of 0.921% (1 in 108.6 corpses) to drop from [[Frozen Corpses|Vanguard Corpses]].
If the player has at least [[Grandpa Wolf|Frozen Corpse Milestone]] VI, [[Heart of the Mountain|Gifts from the Departed]] 101 (with a [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]) and all [[Gemstone Crystals|crystals]] collected, the chance is 1.18% (1 in 84.5 corpses).
Shattered Locket can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].

# Shelled Ammonite Skin
Categories: Legendary items, Pet Skins, Fire Sales, Pages with UIs, Article stubs

Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Ammonite pets.; This skin will allow you to swap between 14 shell themed ammonites!; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; LEGENDARY COSMETIC
The Shelled Ammonite Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Shelled Ammonite Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on August 22, 2026. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 3,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Shelled Ammonite Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Ammonite Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 14 different versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 14 shell themed ammonites!

# Shen's Regalia
Aliases: Shens Regalia
Categories: Legendary items, Accessories
  This article documents Shen's Regalia. For the Bracelet required to access the [[Cerebral Citadel]], see [[Shen's Ringalia]].
[Shen's Regalia]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Shen's Auction]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SHENS_REGALIA
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +16; According to the rumors, Shen himself is crafting special items. Have this in your inventory to gain access to a unique room hidden below the Auction House.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; LEGENDARY ACCESSORY
Shen's Regalia is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that gives the player access to the [[Regalia Room]].
## Obtaining
### Shen's Auction
The Shen's Regalia appears in [[Shen's Auction]], and can be won if the player is one of the top 80 bidders on the item when the auction ends. [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] profiles have a separate auction, where top 40 bidders get the item. When [[Diaz|Mayor Diaz]] is elected with the Volume trading perk, the quantity is doubled to 160 top bids (80 for [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]])
### Raffle of the Century
Shen's Regalia can also be obtained from [[Raffle of the Century]] event. If obtained that way, the player can enter the [[Regalia Room]] even without [[Museum|Museum Milestone 10]], which would normally be required to participate in [[Shen's Auction]].
## Usage
If the player has a Shen's Regalia, they can go into the [[Regalia Room]].
Once in [[Regalia Room]], the following items can be purchased:
|Slot|Name|Cost|
|-|-|-|
|#1|Divine Gift I ([[Divine Gift]])|25,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|#2|[[The Art of Peace]]|50,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|#3|[[Blinking Rock Skin]]|50,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|#3|[[Rat Jetpack]]|50,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|#3|[[Surprised Rock Skin]]|50,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|#3|[[Sus Rock Skin]]|50,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|#3|[[Sweating Rock Skin]]|50,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|#3|[[Diana's Bookshelf]]|75,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|#3|[[Necron's Ladder]]|75,000,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The old texture for Shen's Regalia was the second piece of fanart to be added into the game, with the first being the [[Corrupted Fragment]].
  - It was designed by Perpillow to be used a talisman in a theoretical fishing update.

Tooltip: Speed: -100; Gemstones:; [⚔] [⚔] [⚔] [⚔] [⚔]; [⚔] [⚔] [⚔] [⚔] [⚔]; [⚔] [⚔] [⚔] [⚔] [⚔]; [⚔] [⚔] [⚔] [⚔] [⚔]; Ability: Gemstone Exchange The amount of Book-Modifiers able to be applied to this item is doubled, but all gemstones are half as effective.; This item has no purchasing data yet!; This item can be reforged!; LEGENDARY CHESTPLATE
The Shimmersparkle Chestplate is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Chestplates|Chestplate]] that doubles the amount of book modifiers able to be applied while making [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] half as effective. It displays the buyer's name, cost paid, and the details about in which auction it was sold and the ranking of the buyer within that auction. This data disappears if the item is traded in any way.
## Obtaining
Shimmersparkle Chestplate is one of the five items that appear at [[Shen's Auction|Shen's Special]] while [[Diaz]] is elected with her Volume Trading perk and can be bid on by players. The top 20 bidders win the item. [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] profiles have a separate auction, where top 10 bidders get the item.
## Usage
Chestplate:
Shimmersparkle Chestplate [[Speed]]: -100
[Effects]
[Ability: Gemstone Exchange; The amount of Book-Modifiers able to be applied to this item is doubled, but all gemstones are half as effective.]
## Trivia
- Given enough Opal Gemstones, this chestplate has the potential to provide the highest amount of [[True Defense]] in the game.
  - To outclass a [[Sorrow Armor|Sorrow Chestplate]], 2 [[Gemstone|Perfect Opal Gemstone]] and 18 [[Gemstone|Flawless Opal Gemstone]], or 14 [[Gemstone|Perfect Opal Gemstone]] and 1 [[Gemstone|Fine Opal Gemstone]] can be used (at [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity).
  - If 20 [[Gemstone|Perfect Opal Gemstone]] is used, it provides 37 [[True Defense]] over a [[Sorrow Armor|Sorrow Chestplate]] with 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Opal Gemstone]] (assuming both are [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]]).
- The first letters of the items required to unlock the 5th to 20th gemstone slots on this item are supposed to spell out "SHEN IS SUPER COOL". However, due to Salmon Opal being renamed to [[Kaleidoscopic Mineral]], they now spell out "KHEN IS SUPER COOL" instead.
- The total Bazaar material cost to unlock all gemstone slots on the Shimmersparkle Chestplate is (not including 30 [[Sinful Dice]] and 1 [[Recall Potion]]).

# Shiny Rod
Categories: Legendary items, Items, The Uprising, Tools, Other Tools
[Shiny Rod]
Type: Item

[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SHINY_ROD
Tooltip: Shiny pigs run away from you 30%; faster while holding this.; Extract 25% extra coins from shiny orbs.; LEGENDARY
A Shiny Rod is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
A Shiny Rod can be purchased from [[Piggy]] for 128 [[Shiny Token]] during the [[Year of the Pig]].
## Usage
A [[Shiny Rod]] Shiny Rod increases the speed that [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] run away from the player by 30% and increases [[Coins]] from [[Shiny Orb|Shiny Orbs]] by 25% while held.
The Shiny Rod does not need to be held while leading the [[Shiny Pig]] to gain extra [[Coins]], only when extracting the [[Shiny Orb]].
## Trivia
- This item was added as part of YouTuber Technoblade's mayor perks, due to his position as a [[Resistance Leader]].

The Shinyfish Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Help From Above]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fishing
A Shinyfish Shard can be obtained from [[Fishing]] with the [[Attributes|Star Bait]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants a +0.1%–1% chance to fish one up.
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Shinyfish Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Help From Above]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Help From Above ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1.25–12.5 [[Health Regen]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Shinyfish Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||

[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Shinyfish Shard has the following description:
- Their radiant appearance makes them prized by collectors, but their elusive nature ensures they remain a rare and treasured find.
## Trivia
- Despite being a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Shinyfish Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.
- [[Wooden Bait]] can be used to efficiently catch Shinyfish Shards.

The Shleepy Sheep Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that can be purchased from the Hypixel Store.
## Obtaining
The Shleepy Sheep Skin can be purchased from the Hypixel Store in the 2026 Summer Legacy Bundle.
## Usage
The Shleepy Sheep Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Sheep Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 16 different versions.
- This skin is swappable between 16 sleepy variants that change depending on the time of day!

Applied To: [[Belts]]
Apply Cost: 500,000 [[Coins]]

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 20,480x (320 stacks (20,480 items total)) [[Ender Pearl]] 4,096x (64 stacks (4,096 items total)) [[Blaze Powder]] 4x [[Shriveled Wasp]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 4 [[Shriveled Wasp]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SHRIVELED_CORNEA
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Bloodshot reforge when combined with a Belt.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Crit Chance  Crit Damage; Bloodshot Bonus When killing an enemy, you have a 2.5% chance to cocoon them!; Cocooned enemies will quickly hatch, and can be killed again.; (Cannot trigger in Dungeons, Kuudra, or on Bosses).; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 26.; LEGENDARY REFORGE STONE
A Shriveled Cornea is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Bloodshot]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Belts]]. Its recipe is unlocked at [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer VIII (8)]]. It grants [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]] and [[Crit Chance]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Spider Slayer VIII (8) ([[Spider Slayer]])|4x [[Shriveled Wasp]] 64x [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]]|Recipe: [[Shriveled Wasp]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] x64 → Shriveled Cornea|
### Raffle of the Century
Shriveled Cornea can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Shriveled Cornea can be combined with a [[Belts|Belt]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Bloodshot]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]] and [[Crit Chance]], which scale with the Belt's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXVII (27)]]. A Belt that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Bloodshot]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Bloodshot Bonus, which allows the player a 2.5% (1 in 40) to [[Cocooning|Cocoon]] killed mobs. This cannot trigger in [[Dungeons]], [[Kuudra]] or against bosses.

## Trivia
- On release, it was possible to get the [[Reforging|Bloodshot]] reforge on [[Belts]] via the [[Blacksmith]].
  - This was patched by effectively removing all applied [[Reforging|Bloodshot]] reforges from items (by making the bloodshot modifier a no-op and changing newly applied reforges to blood_shot). Players who applied their reforges legitimately were refunded.
- Despite it being possible to apply [[Reforging|Bloodshot]] to a [[Safari Belt]], [[Cocooning]] does not work in the [[Critter Safari]].

# Signal Enhancer
Categories: Legendary items, Items
[Signal Enhancer]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 5,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SIGNAL_ENHANCER
Tooltip: A marvelous piece of technology, rumored to enhance the capabilities of a beacon.; LEGENDARY
A Signal Enhancer is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that can be obtained from [[Tree Gifts]].
## Obtaining
A Signal Enhancer has base 0% chance to drop from [[Tree Gifts]]. This chance can be increased by +0.1% per level of [[Attributes|Beacon Zealot]], up to 1%.
## Usage
### Tune Beacons
A Signal Enhancer can be consumed to tune the [[Moonglade Beacon]] or [[Torrhus Beacon]]. Tuning the beacon in this way results in tuning 2 panels at once. This can be done 10 times.
Successfully tuning a beacon with a Signal Enhancer grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+2 SkyBlock XP]], +0.5 [[Foraging Fortune]] of the relevant fortune stats from the beacon, increases the total amount of stacks that can affect the player, and grants an increased chance to obtain [[Beaconmite Shard|Beaconmite Shards]] from that beacon. The Signal Enhancer is consumed in the process.
The specific fortune stats granted are as follows:
- [[Moonglade Beacon]]: +0.5 [[Fig Fortune]] and +0.5 [[Mangrove Fortune]] per tier
- [[Torrhus Beacon]]: +0.5 [[Helix Fortune]] per tier
### Salvaging
A Signal Enhancer can be salvaged for 5 [[Forest Essence]].
## History

|Jun 5, 2025|Added Signal Enhancer.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Signal Enhancer as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
|Sep 30, 2026|Signal Enhancers can now be salvaged for 5 [[Forest Essence]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Signal Enhancer as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

The Silk-Edge Sword is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Leaping Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Leaping Sword]] 1x [[Silky Lichen]] 24x [[Enchanted Fermented Spider Eye]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Fermented Spider Eye]] x6 ×4 + [[Silky Lichen]] + [[Leaping Sword]] → Silk-Edge Sword|
## Usage
Sword:
Silk-Edge Sword [[Damage]]: +175 [[Strength]]: +125 [[Crit Damage]]: +25
[Effects]
[Ability: Leap RIGHT CLICK; Leap into the air and deal 400 damage to nearby enemies upon landing on the ground. Damaged enemies will also be frozen for 1 second.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 50; Cooldown: 1s; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 1]
The Silk-Edge Sword's Leap ability allows the player to leap into the air. Upon landing, all enemies within a 4-block radius will receive 400 damage and be stunned for 1s. The ability costs 50 [[Mana]] and has a 1s cooldown.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Spider Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|20|20|
|✪|5|25|
|✪✪|10|35|
|✪✪✪|15|50|
|✪✪✪✪|20; 10,000 [[Coins]]|70; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|25; 25,000 [[Coins]]|95; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- Killing a [[Mob]] using Silk-Edge Sword's or [[Leaping Sword]]'s ability for the first time awards the [[Player]] with the [[Achievements|"Death From Above" Achievement]].
- The Silk-Edge Sword was obtainable prior to its implementation due to a bug that rewarded the [[Silky Lichen]] from the [[End Race]].
- Players used to be able to cheese [[Livid]]'s boss fight in [[The Catacombs - Floor V|Floor V]] of Dungeons by spamming the Silk-Edge Sword's ability on him to prevent him from cloning himself, allowing the real Livid to be revealed.
  - This method was patched on the release of [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Floor VI]].
- The Silk-Edge Sword and [[Leaping Sword]] have separate cooldowns, allowing players to use both abilities simultaneously.
- The Silk-Edge Sword references the Spider class in [[References to other Hypixel minigames|Mega Walls]].

### Dark Auction

A Skeleton Key, also referred to as Vanguard Key, is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
A Skeleton Key can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain X (10)]]. It requires 1 [[Perfect Plate]] and 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] and takes 30m to forge.

  - 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] 3 [[Glacite Jewel]]
### Frozen Corpses
Skeleton Keys can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].
|Type|Quantity|Chance Per Roll|

|Vanguard|[[Skeleton Key]] 1 Skeleton Key|0.28% (1 in 351.5)|

Skeleton Key can be used in [[Glacite Mineshafts]] to open [[Frozen Corpses|Vanguard Corpses]]' backpacks. The item will be consumed after use.

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Grants +1.1 Treasure Chance.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; LEGENDARY PET ITEM
Snake Eyes are a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv517]|[[Titanoboa]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.11%|
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Snake Eyes will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +1.1 [[Treasure Chance]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Snake Eyes for 500,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
## Trivia
- Snake Eyes is a reference to a specific dice roll in the game of craps, specifically when both dice land on 1: as a result, the Snake Eyes grant +1.1 [[Treasure Chance]] rather than just +1 .

### Crafting

## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Sorrow Helmet ([[Sorrow Armor]]); [[True Defense]]: +50 [[Magic Find]]: +5 [[Mining Speed]]: +50 [[Mining Fortune]]: +20|Sorrow Chestplate ([[Sorrow Armor]]); [[True Defense]]: +80 [[Magic Find]]: +5 [[Mining Speed]]: +50 [[Mining Fortune]]: +20|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Sorrow Leggings ([[Sorrow Armor]]); [[True Defense]]: +70 [[Magic Find]]: +5 [[Mining Speed]]: +50 [[Mining Fortune]]: +20|Sorrow Boots ([[Sorrow Armor]]); [[True Defense]]: +40 [[Magic Find]]: +5 [[Mining Speed]]: +50 [[Mining Fortune]]: +20|
[Total]
[Sorrow Armor True Defense: +240 Magic Find: +20 Mining Speed: +200 Mining Fortune: +80]
[Effects]
[2-Piece Set Bonus: Mist Aura; Hides the wearer in a guise of mist, multiplying damage from ghosts by 0.6x.]
Sorrow Armor grants a significant amount of [[True Defense]]. Additionally, its Mist Aura 2-piece set bonus grants [[Invisibility Potion|Invisibility]] and multiplies damage from [[Ghost|Ghosts]] by 0.6x, reducing damage taken from [[Ghost|Ghosts]].
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sorrowful Samurai Skin]]|[[Sorrowful Samurai Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - November 1, 2025 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin will allow you to swap between 18 tearful versions!|
|[[Sorrowful Samurai Skin]]|[[Sorrowful Samurai Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - November 1, 2025 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
|[[Paladin Skin]]|[[Paladin Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Event Shop]] - Halloween 2024 - 150 Event Silver||
## Trivia
- The [[Invisibility Potion|Invisibility]] effect granted by the Mist Aura bonus can be disabled in the [[Booster Cookie]] menu.
- Equipping Sorrow Armor overrides the timer for the [[Invisibility Potion|Invisibility]] effect, as un-equipping it clears the player of that effect regardless of another source.
- Sorrow Armor gives the most base [[Magic Find]] and [[True Defense]] in the game.
- Sorrow Armor and [[Yog Armor]] are the only armor sets that have Universal Gemstone Slots.
  - The ability to have [[Gemstone|Opal]] Gemstones combined with its high [[True Defense]] makes it a popular pick for early-game [[Blaze Slayer]].
- Sorrow Armor, [[Yog Armor]] and [[Armor of Divan]] are the only armor sets that can hold [[Gemstone|Topaz]] Gemstones.

ID-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes

[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: SPIKE
Tooltip: Payback I When hit, your next hit deals +2%; damage; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 20.; LEGENDARY WATER SHARD (ID L14)
The Spike Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Payback]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Spike Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Spike]] with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]].
### Huntraps
A Spike Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Murkwater Depths]] (Underwater) or [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Spike Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Spike Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Payback]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Payback ([[Attributes]]); When hit, your next hit deals +2%–20% damage.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Spike Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Spike Shard has the following description:
- When startled, these fish inflate rapidly, transforming into spiked balls that deter predators with their sharp quills and toxic skin.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPINE_FOSSIL
Tooltip: A well-preserved set of neural spines. Whatever possessed these was an adept swimmer.; LEGENDARY
A Spine Fossil is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that can be obtained by putting [[Suspicious Scrap]] into the [[Fossil Excavator]] along with a [[Chisel]].
## Usage
The Spine Fossil can be donated to [[Dr. Stone]] to unlock the recipe for the [[Spinosaurus Pet]].
It can be used alongside [[Gemstone|Flawless Aquamarine Gemstone]] to make a [[Spinosaurus Pet]] in [[The Forge]] after 7 days.
Spine Fossils can also be used at a [[Jotraeline Greatforge]] or [[Geonathan Greatforge]] to add 50,000 Fuel to a [[Drills|Drill]].

The Spinosaurus Pet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Fishing]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
The Spinosaurus Pet can be forged in [[The Forge]] with 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Aquamarine Gemstone]] and 1 [[Spine Fossil]] for 7 days after donating [[Spine Fossil]] to [[Dr. Stone]]. It requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] to forge.

The Spirit Plushie Lion Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining

# Spirit Shortbow
Aliases: ⚚ Spirit Shortbow,  Spirit Shortbow
Categories: Legendary items, Weapons, Catacombs Loot, Craftable items, Bows, Catacombs, Dungeons, Shortbows
[Spirit Shortbow]
Type: Weapon ([[Weapons]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Dungeon ([[Dungeons]]) Completion: [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]
Stats: [[Damage]]: 210; [[Strength]]: 100
Shot Cooldown: 0.75s
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ITEM_SPIRIT_BOW
Tooltip: Gear Score: 350 (381); Damage: +210 (+231); Strength: +100 (+110); Shot Cooldown: 0.75s; Deals +2% damage to  Undead Mobs for every 1% of your missing health.; Spirit Item: When turned into a ghost, this item becomes a Ghost Ability.; Spirit Ability: Spirit Bomb Shoots a spirit that deals 8000; damage on impact.; Shortbow: Instantly shoots!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor IV Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON BOW
The Spirit Shortbow is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Shortbows|Shortbow]]. It is unlocked at completion of [[The Catacombs - Floor IV|Floor IV]].
## Obtaining
### Thorn Collection
The Spirit Shortbow can be claimed once as a reward from [[Thorn|Thorn III (3)]], which is unlocked after killing [[Thorn]] 150 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Spirit Shortbow can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]])|Obsidian|4,000,000 [[Coins]]|1.2195% (1 in 82 runs)|2.2066% (1 in 45.3 runs)|260|1|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|4,000,000 [[Coins]]|1.3841% (1 in 72.3 runs)|2.4506% (1 in 40.8 runs)|260|4|
## Usage
Sword:
Spirit Shortbow [[Damage]]: +210 [[Strength]]: +100
[Effects]
[Deals +2% damage to Undead (Undead (Mob Type)) Mobs for every 1% of your missing health.; Spirit Item: When turned into a ghost, this item becomes a Ghost Ability. Ability: Spirit Bomb; Shoots a spirit that deals 8,000 damage on impact.]
Sword:
[[Spirit Shortbow]] [[Damage]]: +220 [[Strength]]: +110
[Effects]
[Deals +2% damage to Undead (Undead (Mob Type)) Mobs for every 1% of your missing health.; Spirit Item: When turned into a ghost, this item becomes a Ghost Ability. Ability: Spirit Bomb; Shoots a spirit that deals 8,000 damage on impact.]
The Spirit Shortbow deals +2% more damage to [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] mobs per 1% [[Health]] the player is missing. While in the [[Catacombs]], the Spirit Shortbow gains the Spirit Bomb ability: this ability fires a spirit, dealing 8000 damage on impact.
## Upgrading
A Spirit Shortbow can be upgraded into Spirit Shortbow using 8 [[Thorn Fragment|Thorn Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +10 [[Damage]]
- +10 [[Strength]]
- -0.05s Shot Cooldown
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Spirit Shortbow]] 1x Spirit Shortbow 8x [[Thorn Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Thorn Fragment]] ×8 + Spirit Shortbow → [[Spirit Shortbow]]|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|20|20|
|✪✪|50|70|
|✪✪✪|100|170|
|✪✪✪✪|200; 10,000 [[Coins]]|370; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|300; 25,000 [[Coins]]|670; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The Spirit Shortbow's Spirit Bomb ability works while it is in the player's [[Ender Chest]].
- The Spirit Bomb ability is a reference to Dragon Ball's Genki Dama ability.
- The Spirit Shortbow has the longest base cooldown of any [[Shortbows|Shortbow]], at 0.75s (0.7s when upgraded).
- The Spirit Shortbow previously shared a name with the [[Spirit Bow]], even after it was converted to a Shortbow.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

Apply them just like an enchant book using the anvil.; This skin can be applied to any tier of Crimson Helmet, Aurora Helmet, Terror Helmet, Fervor Helmet, and Hollow Helmet.; This skin is swappable between 21; creepy variants!; LEGENDARY COSMETIC
The Spooky Skin is an [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from the Hypixel Store.
## Obtaining
The Spooky Skin could be purchased from the Hypixel Store during a 2026 Hypixel Store Halloween promotion.
## Usage
The Spooky Skin can be applied to a [[Crimson Armor|Crimson Helmet]], [[Aurora Armor|Aurora Helmet]], [[Terror Armor|Terror Helmet]], [[Fervor Armor|Fervor Helmet]] or [[Hollow Armor|Hollow Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 21 different versions.
- This skin is swappable between 21 creepy variants!

# Squeaky Mousemat
Aliases: Mousemat, Squeaky Mousepad, Yaw
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Mob Drops, Tools
[Squeaky Mousemat]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SQUEAKY_MOUSEMAT
Tooltip: Ability: Set RIGHT CLICK Set your yaw and pitch.; Ability: Snap LEFT CLICK Look in the direction of your selected yaw and pitch while in The Garden.; Cooldown: 3s; Selected Yaw: 0; Selected Pitch: 0; Found at the bottom of a vacuum bag, this furry mousemat comes complete with a cute pair of ears!; LEGENDARY
A Squeaky Mousemat is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Field Mouse]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%* ([[Pests]])|
## Usage
Item:
Squeaky Mousemat
[Effects]
[Ability: Set RIGHT CLICK; Set your yaw and pitch. Ability: Snap LEFT CLICK; Look in your direction of your selected yaw and pitch while in The Garden.; Cooldown: 3s]
Using Set with right-click allows the player to save a yaw and pitch to the Squeaky Mousemat. Using Snap with left-click snaps the player's camera to the saved yaw and pitch.
## Tips
- For default [[The Garden]] plots, the following pitch and yaw values are recommended:
  - [[Wheat]], [[Carrot]], [[Potato]], and [[Nether Wart]] work well with a pitch of 30 and a yaw of either 90 or -90.
  - [[Mushroom|Red Mushrooms]] and [[Mushroom|Brown Mushrooms]] work well with a pitch of 0 and a yaw of either 75 or 105.
  - [[Cocoa Beans]] work well with a pitch of -75 and a yaw of 0.
  - [[Melon|Melons]] and [[Pumpkin|Pumpkins]] work well with a pitch of 40 and a yaw of either 40 or 140.
  - [[Cactus]] works well with a pitch of 0 and a yaw of either 90 or -90.
  - [[Sugar Cane]], [[Sunflower]], [[Moonflower]], and [[Wild Rose]] work well with a pitch of 0 and a yaw of either 40 or 140.
## Trivia
- Squeaky Mousemat changes the way the player is facing by teleporting them to the same location, and setting the way they're facing, making it a little janky if the player is moving when using the item.

The Starborn Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Echo of Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Starborn Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] and Elemental Family
- Second: [[Galaxy Fish Shard]]
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Starborn Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Echo of Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Echo of Elemental ([[Attributes]]); Buffs all other shards in the Elemental Family by +2%–20%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

Tooltip: Health: +70; Defense: +110; Strength: +25; Gemstones: []; Full Set Bonus: Strong Blood (0\ 4); Improves Aspect of the End ⋗ +75 Damage; Instant Transmission:; ⋗ +2 teleport range ⋗ +3 seconds ⋗ +5 Strength on cast; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 16.; LEGENDARY HELMET
Strong Dragon Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set that is focused on improving [[Strength]]. It is obtained from the [[Ender Dragon|Strong Dragon]] or crafted with 240 (3 stacks plus 48) [[Dragon Fragment|Strong Dragon Fragments]]. Its Full Set Bonus buffs the [[Aspect of the End]]'s [[Damage]] and Ability. The bonuses provided from a full set of [[Strong Dragon Armor]] Strong Dragon Armor that affect the [[Aspect of the End]] also apply to the [[Aspect of the Void]].
[[Combat|Combat XVI (16)]] is required to be able to use this set and, if the item is a dungeon item, [[Catacombs|Catacombs XII (12)]] is required to be able to use this set while in [[Dungeons]].
## Obtaining
### Strong Dragons
Strong Dragon Armor pieces can be dropped from the [[Ender Dragon|Strong Dragon]]. One piece has a 30% chance to drop from the Dragon if the player meets the [[Ender Dragon|Dragon Weight]] requirement.
Weight Requirements:
- Helmet: 295 Dragon Weight
- Chestplate: 410 Dragon Weight
- Leggings: 360 Dragon Weight
- Boots: 290 Dragon Weight
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Strong Dragon Helmet ([[Strong Dragon Armor]])|None|50x Strong Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×5 → [[Strong Dragon Armor]]|
|Strong Dragon Chestplate ([[Strong Dragon Armor]])|None|80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Strong Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×8 → [[Strong Dragon Armor]]|
|Strong Dragon Leggings ([[Strong Dragon Armor]])|None|70x (1 stack plus 6 (70 in total)) Strong Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×7 → [[Strong Dragon Armor]]|
|Strong Dragon Boots ([[Strong Dragon Armor]])|None|40x Strong Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×4 → [[Strong Dragon Armor]]|

## Tips
- Getting a [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Helmet]] with this armor is a better option due to damage. However, the Full Set Bonus will be sacrificed.
- The chestplate can substitute a [[Shadow Assassin Armor|Shadow Assassin]] or [[Necron's Armor|Necron's Chestplate]] for players on a budget. A [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Helmet]] with these sets is a worthwhile option for headgear.
## Trivia
- This armor is one of the four dragon armors that have coloring similar to that of the main stat bonus. The reddish colors of the armor (specifically the Chestplate) has the same red color of the [[Strength]] stat. The other three armors that have similar colors to their stats are [[Wise Dragon Armor]], [[Young Dragon Armor]], and [[Holy Dragon Armor]].
- A piece of this armor reforged to [[Reforging|Strong]] becomes "Very Strong" Dragon Armor.
  - Very Strong Dragon Armor is no longer obtainable as the Strong Reforge was removed.

The Sun Fish Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Starborn]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Sun Fish Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Shinyfish Shard]]
- Second: [[Bezal Shard]], [[Lava Flame Shard]], [[Fire Eel Shard]], [[Bal Shard]], [[Flare Shard]], [[Cinderbat Shard]], or [[Flaming Spider Shard]]
||
||

[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Sun Fish Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Starborn]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Starborn ([[Attributes]]); The effect of [[Starborn Temples]] is +1%–10% stronger on you.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Tooltip: Farming Fortune: +12; While breaking crops with your fist:; Grants x2  Overbloom Capped at 500 Farming Fortune Crops automatically replanted; A farmer of legend, said to be born of the sun's grace itself. With bare hands he cultivated the earth, and from his harvests baked humble bread bowls, gifted only to those held close to his heart.; Right-click to equip!; This item can be reforged!; LEGENDARY GLOVES
The Sun's Grasp is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Gloves|Glove]] that grants multiple bonuses when the player breaks crops with their fist.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Ray of Helios]] 3x [[Godseed]] 3x [[All-in Aloe]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Compacted Sunflower]]|Recipe: [[Godseed]] ×3 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x64 ×2 + [[Ray of Helios]] + [[All-in Aloe]] ×3 → Sun's Grasp|
## Usage
Gloves:
Sun's Grasp [[Farming Fortune]]: +12
[Effects]
[While breaking crops with your fist: Grants 2x Overbloom Caps Farming Fortune at 500 Farming Fortune Automatically replants crops]
The Sun's Grasp grants +12 [[Farming Fortune]], alongside several bonuses when breaking crops with a fist.
- Grants 2x [[Overbloom]]
- Caps [[Farming Fortune]] at 500
- Automatically replants crops
The Sun's Grasp prohibits the breaking of crops with anything other than the fist when equipped.
## Trivia
- The Sun's Grasp is one of a few recipes hidden from the [[Recipe Book|Recipes]] menu.
- Applying the [[Reforging|Lunar]] reforge to the Sun's Grasp causes its name to change to Eclipse's Grasp.

Tooltip: Health: +90; Defense: +130; Strength: +10; Crit Chance: +2%; Crit Damage: +10%; Intelligence: +25; Speed: +3; Gemstones: [⚔]; Full Set Bonus: Superior Blood (0\ 4); Increases all Combat stats and  Magic Find by 5% and Aspect of the Dragons ability deals 50% more damage.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 20.; LEGENDARY HELMET
Superior Dragon Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set obtained from [[Ender Dragon|Superior Dragons]]. It is the most powerful and expensive of 8 different [[Dragon Armor]] sets.
## Obtaining
### Superior Dragon
Superior Dragon Armor pieces can be dropped from the [[Ender Dragon|Superior Dragon]]. One piece has a 30% chance to drop from the Dragon if the player meets the [[Ender Dragon|Dragon Weight]] requirement.
Weight Requirements:
- Helmet: 295 Dragon Weight
- Chestplate: 410 Dragon Weight
- Leggings: 360 Dragon Weight
- Boots: 290 Dragon Weight
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Superior Dragon Helmet ([[Superior Dragon Armor]])|None|50x Superior Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×5 → [[Superior Dragon Armor]]|
|Superior Dragon Chestplate ([[Superior Dragon Armor]])|None|80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Superior Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×8 → [[Superior Dragon Armor]]|
|Superior Dragon Leggings ([[Superior Dragon Armor]])|None|70x (1 stack plus 6 (70 in total)) Superior Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×7 → [[Superior Dragon Armor]]|
|Superior Dragon Boots ([[Superior Dragon Armor]])|None|40x Superior Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×4 → [[Superior Dragon Armor]]|

Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|

[Total]
[Superior Dragon Armor Health: +450 Defense: +600 Strength: +40 Crit Chance: +8 Crit Damage: +40 Intelligence: +100 Speed: +12]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Superior Blood; Increases all Combat stats and Magic Find by 5% and Aspect of the Dragons ability deals 50% more damage.]
[[Combat|Combat XX (20)]] is required to be able to use this set and, if the item is a dungeon item, [[Catacombs|Catacombs XIV (14)]] is required to be able to use this set while in [[Dungeons]].
When the full set is worn, the player's Combat stats and [[Magic Find]] are increased by 5%.

## Tips
- A full set of [[Reforging|Renowned]] Superior Dragon Armor provides more [[Magic Find]] than a full set of [[Reforging|Renowned]] [[Sorrow Armor]] if the player has more than 416 [[Magic Find]] while having no armor equipped.
## Trivia
- When this armor set was [[Reforging|Reforged]] to "[[Reforging|Superior]]", it became "Highly Superior". This reforge is no longer obtainable.
- The eyebrows on this set are different from the other sets, giving it a more aggressive facial expression.
- Superior Dragon Helmet is the first armor piece to have 2 cosmetic skins - Shimmer and Baby.
- Combined with a level 100 [[Ender Dragon Pet]], the [[Reforging|Renowned]] reforge, and [[Legion|Legion V]] on all 4 pieces with 20 nearby players, a stat boost of 47% can be obtained.

Tooltip: Combat Pet; Strength: +0.75➡75; Crit Chance: +0.05➡5%; Ferocity: +0.25➡25; Close Combat Deal 1➡100% more damage to enemies within 1.5 blocks.; Ferocious Roar Attacks have a 0.1➡10% chance to stun the target (10s cooldown).; Tyrant Combat stats granted by pet items on this pet are increased by 1➡100%.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; LEGENDARY
The T-Rex Pet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
The T-Rex Pet can be forged in [[The Forge]] with 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Onyx Gemstone]] and 1 [[Footprint Fossil]] for 7 days after donating [[Footprint Fossil]] to [[Dr. Stone]]. It requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[T-Rex Pet]] 1 T-Rex Pet
  - 1 [[Footprint Fossil]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Onyx Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]]

Tentacle Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
A Tentacle Dye has a variable chance of dropping after defeating [[Kuudra]].
- 0.001% (1 in 100,000) chance from [[Kuudra]] Basic Tier
- 0.00125% (1 in 80,000) chance from [[Kuudra]] Hot Tier
- 0.0016% (1 in 60,000) chance from [[Kuudra]] Burning Tier
- 0.0025% (1 in 40,000) chance from [[Kuudra]] Fiery Tier
- 0.005% (1 in 20,000) chance from [[Kuudra]] Infernal Tier
## Usage
Tentacle Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #324D6C. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TERMINATOR
Tooltip: Damage: +310; Strength: +50; Crit Damage: +250%; Attack Speed: +40%; Shot Cooldown: 0.5s; Shoots 3 arrows at once.; Can damage endermen.; Divides your  Crit Chance by 4!; Ability: Salvation LEFT CLICK Can be cast after landing 3 hits.; Shoot a beam, penetrating up to 5; enemies.; The beam always crits.; Soulflow Cost: 1⸎; Shortbow: Instantly shoots!; This item can be reforged!; ☠ Requires Enderman Slayer 7.; LEGENDARY BOW
The Terminator is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Shortbows|Shortbow]]. It is unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Judgement Core]] 3x [[Null Blade]] 4x [[Braided Griffin Feather]] 8x [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Tarantula Silk]]|Recipe: [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] x4 ×2 + [[Null Blade]] ×3 + [[Tarantula Silk]] x64 ×2 + [[Judgement Core]] + [[Braided Griffin Feather]] x4 → Terminator|
## Usage
Bow:
Terminator [[Damage]]: +310 [[Strength]]: +50 [[Crit Damage]]: +250% [[Attack Speed]]: +40%
[Effects]
[Shoots 3 arrows at once.; Can damage endermen.; Divides your Crit Chance by 4! Shortbow: Instantly Shoots!; Shot Cooldown: 0.5s Ability: Salvation LEFT CLICK; Can be casted after landing 3 hits.; Shoot a beam, penetrating up to 5 enemies.; The beam always crits.; Soulflow Cost (Soulflow (Mechanic)): 1; Cooldown: 0.25s]
The Terminator is a shortbow that is capable of hitting [[Enderman|Endermen]]. It fires 3 arrows per shot: 1 travels in the direction the player is looking at, and 2 arrows fire at offset angles to either side. Spread is greatest when aiming horizontally and decreases as the player looks up or down.
After landing 3 hits on enemies, the Salvation ability can be cast with Left Click, costing 1 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] and having a 0.25s cooldown. The beam has a range of 32 blocks, can hit up to 5 enemies, and is guaranteed to crit. While the Terminator is being held and the Salvation ability is not on its cooldown, a colored text indicator appears on the action bar, indicating when the ability can be used.
- T1 displays when 1 hit has been landed.
- T2 displays when 2 hits have been landed.
- T3! displays when 3 hits have been landed and the ability is ready to be used.
The Terminator divides the player's [[Crit Chance]] by 4.
## Fire Rate
When left-clicking, the fire rate starts at 2.22 shots per second (9 ticks per shot) with 0 [[Attack Speed]] and increases to 5 shots per second (4 ticks per shot) with 100 [[Attack Speed]]. The increase in fire rate with [[Attack Speed]] is incremental, not continuous. The maximum possible fire rate that can be achieved by optimal left-clicking depending on the player's [[Attack Speed]]:
|Ticks per shot|[[Attack Speed]] Lowest|[[Attack Speed]] Highest|Shots per second|
|-|-|-|-|
|9 (0.45s)|< -8|11|2.22|
|7 (0.35s)|12|40|2.86|
|5 (0.25s)|41|66|4.00|
|4 (0.20s)|67|100|5.00|
## Essence Crafting
This weapon can be upgraded with Dragon Essence. Once converted, it requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs XXVII (27)]] to use while in [[Catacombs]].
Essence Required; [[Dragon Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|300|300|
|✪|100|400|
|✪✪|200|600|
|✪✪✪|300|900|
|✪✪✪✪|500; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,400; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|750; 25,000 [[Coins]]|2,150; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The Terminator's crafting recipe was changed after it was added, adding the [[Judgement Core]] to its recipe.
▦ Recipe
Recipe: [[Null Blade]] x3 ×2 + [[Tarantula Silk]] x64 ×2 + [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] + [[Braided Griffin Feather]]
- Prior to update 0.16.1, the Terminator required 3 stacked [[Null Blade|Null Blades]]: since [[Null Blade|Null Blades]] take the model of [[Shears]], which are unstackable, the Terminator could only be crafted through Quick Craft and was exclusive to ranked Hypixel players.
- The [[Archer]]'s Doubleshot passive only affects the middle arrow.
- Prior to Update 0.19, the Terminator required the highest amount of raw materials to craft in the game, at 14,450,332 raw materials.
  - The earliest version of the crafting recipe required 20,866,742 raw materials.
  - As of 0.19, the [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minion XI]] and [[SOS Flare]] requires more amount of raw materials, at 303,596,064 and 148,659,281 raw materials respectively.
- The Terminator's Salvation ability can hit [[Decoy|Decoys]] and [[Thorn]], but cannot damage them.

# Terra Mammoth Skin
Categories: Legendary items, Pet Skins, Fire Sales, Pages with UIs

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Mammoth pets.; This skin will allow you to swap between 19 earthy mammoths!; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; LEGENDARY COSMETIC
The Terra Mammoth Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Terra Mammoth Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on May 30, 2026. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 2,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Terra Mammoth Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Mammoth Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 19 different versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 19 earthy mammoths!

Tooltip: Health: +160; Defense: +50; Crit Damage: +50%; Intelligence: +15; Speed: +12; Gemstones: [⚔] [⚔]; Tiered Bonus: Hydra Strike (0\ 4); Every 0.2s, arrow attacks grant 1; stack of ⁑ Hydra Strike. Lose 1 stack after 4s of not gaining a stack.; Each stack grants +2% Damage and +1%; Arrow Speed.; At 10 stacks shoot +2 arrows that deal 20%; Arrow Damage.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; ❣ Requires Kuudra Basic Tier Completion.; LEGENDARY HELMET
Terror Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set. There are 4 upgraded tiers of Terror Armor: Hot, Burning, Fiery, and Infernal, which can all be obtained by prestiging the previous tier.
## Obtaining
Basic Tier Terror Armor Pieces can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Paid Reward Chests]] at the end of a [[Kuudra]] fight.
Piece Drop Chances:
- Basic Tier: 4.31%
- Hot Tier: 4.2%
- Burning Tier: 4.05%
- Fiery Tier: 3.91%
- Infernal Tier: 4.08%
Higher tiers of Terror Armor can be obtained by prestiging the previous tier at a blacksmith ( [[Malik]], [[Igor]], [[Alwin]], or [[The Hex]]), which can be done if it has 10 [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] applied to it. After being prestiged, Armor pieces lose their Stars.

[Tiered Bonus: Hydra Strike; Every 0.2s, arrow attacks grant 1 stack of Hydra Strike ⁑.; Each Hydra Strike ⁑ stack grants +5% Damage➜+10% Damage➜+15% Damage and +3% Arrow Speed.; At 10 stacks, shoot +2 arrows that deal 60% Arrow Damage.; Lose 1 stack after 4s➜7s➜10s of not gaining a stack.; Pieces Required: 2+]
Upon having at least 2 pieces equipped, the Tiered Set Bonus: Hydra Strike is unlocked. When hitting enemies with arrows, 1 stack of Hydra Strike is gained, but they cannot be gained more than once every 0.2s. Upon reaching 10 stacks, two additional arrows are fired. These arrows inherit most of the properties of the bow it was fired at; for example, a Juju Shortbow's Hydra Strike arrows can damage endermen and cause an AOE effect that hits up to 3 mobs upon striking an enemy. These arrows gain extra damage based on how many pieces of Terror Armor are worn and what tier they are:
|Terror|Infernal|Fiery|Burning|Hot|Basic|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|4/4|+15%|+12.75%|+10.5%|+8.25%|+6%|
|3/4|+10%|+8.5%|+7%|+5.5%|+4%|
|2/4|+5%|+4.25%|+3.5%|+2.75%|+2%|
The damage of the extra arrows increase based on the lowest tier of Terror Armor being worn. Each stack increases some of the player's stats by a certain amount, up to ten stacks:
- Basic Tier: +1% Arrow Speed, 20% side arrow damage
- Hot Tier: +1.5% Arrow Speed, 30% side arrow damage
- Burning Tier: +2% Arrow Speed, 40% side arrow damage
- Fiery Tier: +2.5% Arrow Speed, 50% side arrow damage
- Infernal Tier: +3% Arrow Speed, 60% side arrow damage

|✪|30 Essence|30 Essence|120 Essence|120 Essence|

## Trivia
- The Tiered Bonus interferes with the enchantment [[Enchantments|Overload]], causing the 10%-50% damage bonus to not apply when at max tier and shooting 3 arrows.
- The Tiered Bonus interferes with the [[Spirit Bow]], causing the 2% damage bonus for every 1% of your missing health to undead monsters to not apply when at max tier and shooting 3 arrows.
- The Tiered Bonus interferes with all types of special arrows, causing them to not work.
- The Tiered Bonus interferes with [[Spirit Bow|Spirit Bow (Thorn)]], causing it to not be able to damage thorn when at max tier and shooting 3 arrows
- It has been observed by some community members that the usage of the word “Stacks” and how they function could be a potential reference to the Risk of Rain series.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- A total of 141,005 (2,203 stacks plus 13) [[Crimson Essence]], 45 [[Heavy Pearl]], 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Kuudra Teeth]], and 44,740,000 [[Coins]] are required for one Infernal tier piece, costing .
  - A total of 1,793,505 (28,023 stacks plus 33) [[Crimson Essence]], 97 (1 stack plus 33) [[Heavy Pearl]], and 139,175,000 [[Coins]] are needed from scratch for a single Infernal piece with 15 [[Star Upgrades|Stars]]. An Infernal Terror Helmet additionally requires 440 (6 stacks plus 56) [[Kuudra Teeth]], costing , while an Infernal Terror Chestplate, Leggings, or Boots requires 460 (7 stacks plus 12) [[Kuudra Teeth]], costing .

# Tessellated Ender Pearl
Aliases: Tesselated Ender Pearl
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Enderman Slayer
[Tessellated Ender Pearl]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Absolute Ender Pearl]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 96,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 128,000x (2,000 stacks (128,000 items total)) [[Ender Pearl]] 819,200x (12,800 stacks (819,200 items total)) [[Lapis Lazuli]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TESSELLATED_ENDER_PEARL
Tooltip: LEGENDARY
The Tessellated Ender Pearl is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] crafting item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|32x [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Absolute Ender Pearl]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] x8 ×4 + [[Absolute Ender Pearl]] x16 ×5 → Tessellated Ender Pearl|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Terminator]]|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Judgement Core]] 3x [[Null Blade]] 4x [[Braided Griffin Feather]] [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] 8x Tessellated Ender Pearl 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Tarantula Silk]]|Recipe: Tessellated Ender Pearl x4 ×2 + [[Null Blade]] ×3 + [[Tarantula Silk]] x64 ×2 + [[Judgement Core]] + [[Braided Griffin Feather]] x4 → [[Terminator]]|
|[[Tactical Insertion]]|Blaze Slayer V (5) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|[[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] 4x Tessellated Ender Pearl 8x [[Inferno Vertex]] 48x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: Tessellated Ender Pearl x4 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 + [[Molten Powder]] x48 → [[Tactical Insertion]]|
|[[Draconic Ring]]|None|1x [[Draconic Talisman]] [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] 1x Tessellated Ender Pearl 4x [[Ritual Residue]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Mite Gel]]|Recipe: [[Ritual Residue]] ×4 + [[Mite Gel]] x32 ×3 + [[Draconic Talisman]] + Tessellated Ender Pearl → [[Draconic Ring]]|
|[[Draconic Artifact]]|None|1x [[Draconic Ring]] [[Tessellated Ender Pearl]] 2x Tessellated Ender Pearl 12x [[Ritual Residue]] 128x [[Mite Gel]]|Recipe: [[Ritual Residue]] x3 ×4 + Tessellated Ender Pearl ×2 + [[Mite Gel]] x64 ×2 + [[Draconic Ring]] → [[Draconic Artifact]]|
## Trivia
- It is one of 6 crafting ingredients obtained by combining its raw material 3 times ( [[Ender Pearl]], [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]], [[Absolute Ender Pearl]], [[Tessellated Ender Pearl|Tesselated Ender Pearl]]). The others are the [[Refined Diamond]], the [[Omega Enchanted Egg]], the [[Diamond Magmafish]], and the [[Diamond Lotus]] (and formerly the [[Tightly-Tied Hay Bale]]).
- Its item ID TESSELLATED_ENDER_PEARL was spelled with 2 "L"s, whereas its name "Tesselated Ender Pearl" was displayed with 1 "L". The word "Tesselated" is misspelled, actually being "Tessellated", hence the incident.
- It can be crafted before the [[Absolute Ender Pearl]] can be crafted. This is one of the only cases where a crafting item can be crafted before its predecessor.
- Despite needing stacked Tessellated Ender Pearls in the crafting recipe of [[Terminator]], it was unstackable until Update 0.16.1
- Before being changed to custom items, its texture was a recolored version of [[Absolute Ender Pearl]].
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Jun 22, 2022|Changed name to Tessellated Ender Pearl.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Tessellated Ender Pearl to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Tessellated Ender Pearl as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_TEXTBOOK
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Increases the pet's  Intelligence by 100%.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; LEGENDARY PET ITEM
A Textbook is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that increases a [[Pets|Pet]]'s [[Intelligence]] by 100%. It has no effect on [[Pets]] that do not grant [[Intelligence]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Lapis Lazuli X (10) ([[Lapis Lazuli]])|8x [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] 64x [[Enchanted Paper]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] ×8 + [[Enchanted Paper]] x64 → Textbook|
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Textbook will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it 100% [[Intelligence]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Textbook for 500,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.

# The Art of Peace
Aliases: Art Of Peace, Art of Peace, The Art Of Peace
Categories: Legendary items, Items
Stop quoting my cousin.
— Moon Tzu
[The Art of Peace]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: THE_ART_OF_PEACE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Grants +40 Health when applied to an armor piece. Can only be applied to an item once.; Stop quoting my cousin.; - Moon Tzu; LEGENDARY
The Art of Peace is a Book Modifier. It adds +40 [[Health]] to an [[Armor]] piece.
## Obtaining
The Art of Peace can be bought from the [[Regalia Room]] for 50,000,000 [[Coins]] when it is being sold. Entering [[Regalia Room]] requires [[Museum|Gilded Rank]] and [[Shen's Regalia]].
## Usage
The Art of Peace can be applied to an Armor piece in an [[Anvil]]. Only one can be applied per Armor piece. Items that The Art of Peace is applied to gain +40 [[Health]].
## Trivia
- This item is a reference to the book The Art of War written by the famous Chinese general, strategist, and philosopher, Sun Tzu.
  - It is the complete opposite of [[The Art of War]], with Sun Tzu changing to Moon Tzu, and War changing to Peace.
  - The description "Stop quoting my cousin" is likely a reference to the Youtuber Technoblade's repeated quotations from The Art of War.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 25, 2022|Added The Art of Peace.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added The Art of Peace to the [[Bazaar]].|

The Thermal Thermodynamic Helmet Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that can be obtained from a [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Thermal Thermodynamic Helmet Skin can be purchased from the Summer 2026 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it costs 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Thermal Thermodynamic Helmet Skin can be applied to a [[Thermodynamic Armor|Thermodynamic Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 19 different versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 19 Thermal variants!

## Tips
- Thermodynamic Armor heavily outclasses [[Young Dragon Armor]] for [[Speed]]. However, it cannot be dungeonized and does not affect the [[Speed]] cap.
- Thermodynamic Armor's increased [[Attack Speed]] cap is extra effective with [[Shortbows]], such as the [[Juju Shortbow]] or [[Terminator]].
- Thermodynamic Armor can outclass up to Fiery tier [[Terror Armor]] in terms of DPS.[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
## Trivia
- Thermodynamic Armor grants the most [[Attack Speed]] in the game.
  - Additionally, it is the only [[Armor]] that grants [[Attack Speed]] as a base stat.
- Assuming 0 [[Overbloom]], a player will acquire the 1,024 (16 stacks) [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] before the 128 (2 stacks) [[Cane Knot]] if they have at least 1,038 [[Farming Fortune]].

Tooltip: Consume this item to unlock the Three Stone Jerries Barn Skin on The Garden!; Click to consume!; LEGENDARY COSMETIC
The Three Stone Jerries Barn Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Barn Skins|Barn Skin]] that can be obtained from a [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Three Stone Jerries Barn Skin can be purchased from the Spring 2026 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it costs 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Three Stone Jerries Barn Skin can be consumed on the [[The Garden|Garden]] and then used at [[The Garden|The Desk]] to change the look of the center plot.
## History

|Mar 21, 2026|Added Three Stone Jerries Barn Skin.|

## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Tiamat Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Caiman Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher and Combat Category

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Tiamat Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]]); Your "Echo" Attributes are +5%–50% stronger.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Tier Boost
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Craftable items, Pet items, Pets
[Tier Boost]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 256,000x (4,000 stacks (256,000 items total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 1x [[Tier Boost Core]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_TIER_BOOST
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Boosts the rarity of your pet by 1; tier!; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; LEGENDARY PET ITEM
Tier Boost is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that increases a [[Pets|Pet]]'s [[Rarity]] by 1.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Tier Boost Core]] 10x [[Enchanted Iron Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Block]] x2 ×5 + [[Tier Boost Core]] → Tier Boost|
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Tier Boost will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it increases the [[Rarity]] of the [[Pets|Pet]] by a tier. It cannot increase a [[Rarity]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], with the exception of the [[Bingo Pet]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Tier Boost for 500,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
- Buff
- COMMON ([[Rarity]])→UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
- UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])→RARE ([[Rarity]])
- RARE ([[Rarity]])→EPIC ([[Rarity]])
- EPIC ([[Rarity]])→LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
- LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])→MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]); ( [[Bingo Pet]] only)
## Trivia
- If a player manages to apply a [[Tier Boost]] Tier Boost to a base [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] pet through a bug, it will remain [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rather than becoming [[Rarity|DIVINE]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Tier Boost.|

# Timestalk
Aliases: Time Stalk, Time stalk
Categories: Legendary items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Timestalk]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TIMESTALK
Tooltip: The timestalk is a mysterious plant that defies time itself, starting fully grown and slowly aging backwards.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; LEGENDARY MUTATION
A Timestalk is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Timestalk can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Stoplight Petal]], 2 [[Chorus Fruit]], and 2 [[Shellfruit]] within 1 block of [[End Stone]].
While growing, a Timestalk needs water and has 14 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it increases [[Farming]] experience by 30%, retains water by 100%, and reduces yield by -20%.
When harvested, a Timestalk spawns a [[Timestalk Clone]]. Upon being defeated, the Clone drops a Timestalk.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Timestalks also drop 2,700 (42 stacks plus 12) [[Cactus]], 3,600 (56 stacks plus 16) [[Sugar Cane]], and +14,400 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Timestalk can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 19,000,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 9,500 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Timestalk grants an additional 4,000 [[Copper]].
### NPC Trading
[[Timestalk]] 1 Timestalk is needed to purchase a [[Rose Dragon Pet|Rose Dragon Egg]] from [[Ludleth]].
## Gallery
- Timestalk in the Greenhouse Timestalk in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Timestalk.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Timestalk.|

## Usage
Drill:
Titanium Drill DR-X655 [[Damage]]: +75 [[Breaking Power]]: +9 [[Mining Speed]]: +1,600 [[Mining Fortune]]: +120
The Titanium Drill DR-X655 has a base 3,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]], which can be increased through a [[Drills|Fuel Tank]]. Additionally, a [[Drills|Drill Engine]] and [[Drills|Upgrade Module]] can be added to the drill through a [[Drill Anvil]] to grant further bonuses.
When breaking blocks, the Titanium Drill DR-X655's [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] will deplete. Most resources will remove 1 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] when mined; [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]], [[Gemstone|Onyx]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]] remove 2 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead; [[Glacite]], [[Umber]] and [[Tungsten]] remove 3 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead. [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] can be regained by adding Fuel to the Drill through a [[Drill Anvil]].
### Forging
The Titanium Drill DR-X655, along with a [[Divan's Alloy]] and 50 million [[Coins]], can be used to forge a [[Divan's Drill]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.

## Trivia
- This item was first seen by 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Dueces holding it on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]].
- It takes a total of 3378 hours (140 days and 18 hours) to forge the materials and the drill. It can be decreased with the [[Heart of the Mountain|Quick Forge]] perk and multiple [[The Forge|Forge]] slots to 472.92 hours (19 days and 16.92 hours) or around 681,000 mobs killed on Mining Islands using a [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] with 5 perfect gemstones.
  - The upgrade from [[Titanium Drill DR-X555]] takes 618 hours (25 days and 18 hours) to forge, or 86.52 hours (3 days and 14.52 hours) if included the factors above.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; Is it accounting for 0.20.6?; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
