The Titanium Relic is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that grants +60 [[Mining Speed]]. It can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
The Titanium Relic can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It requires 20 [[Refined Titanium]], 1 [[Titanium Artifact]], and 3 days to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Titanium Relic]] 1 Titanium Relic
  - 20 [[Refined Titanium]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - 1 [[Titanium Artifact]] 12 [[Refined Titanium]] 192 (3 stacks) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 30,720 (480 stacks) [[Titanium]] 1 [[Titanium Ring]] 6 [[Refined Titanium]] 96 (1 stack plus 32) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 15,360 (240 stacks) [[Titanium]] 1 [[Titanium Talisman]] 2 [[Refined Titanium]] 32 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 5,120 (80 stacks) [[Titanium]]
## Trivia
- Upgrading to this accessory will remove all [[The Hex|Modifiers]] applied when it is put in [[The Forge]] to forge it.
- The [[Mining Speed]] of this talisman used to stack with its predecessor variants ( [[Titanium Artifact]], [[Titanium Ring]] and [[Titanium Talisman]]) and gave a total of 150 [[Mining Speed]].
  - This has now been patched.

The Titanoboa Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Bayou Hook]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Titanoboa Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Titanoboa]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Huntraps
A Titanoboa Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Backwater Bayou]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Titanoboa Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Titanoboa Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Bayou Hook]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Bayou Hook ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Double Hook Chance]] while on Fishing Islands.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Titanoboa Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||

[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Titanoboa Shard has the following description:
- The largest species of snake ever proved to exist, it eats a variety of large fish, alligators, and other snakes.
## Trivia
- Despite being a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Titanoboa Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

The Tortoise Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Cloak Improvement]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Tortoise Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Leatherback Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher and Water Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Tortoise Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Cloak Improvement]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Cloak Improvement ([[Attributes]]); Your [[David's Cloak]] gains +1–10 [[True Defense]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Tortoise Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Tortoise Shard has the following description:
- Tortoises can live for over 100 years, their long lifespans making them symbols of resilience and endurance.

The Town Hall Barn Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Barn Skins|Barn Skin]] that could be obtained from a past [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Town Hall Barn Skin could be purchased from the Winter 2023 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. However, due to being before the Winter 2024 Bundle, it cannot appear in future bundles, making it now unobtainable.
## Usage
The Town Hall Barn Skin can be consumed on the [[The Garden|Garden]] and then used at [[The Garden|The Desk]] to change the look of the center plot.

# Treasure Dye
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Treasure Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_TREASURE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #FCD12A!; LEGENDARY DYE
Treasure Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Treasure Dye has a chance to be obtained from [[Treasure|Fishing Treasure]].
|Treasure Tier|Chance|
|-|-|
|GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]])|0.0001% (1 in 1,000,000)|
|GREAT CATCH ([[Treasure]])|0.001% (1 in 100,000)|
|OUTSTANDING CATCH ([[Treasure]])|0.01% (1 in 10,000)|
## Usage
Treasure Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #FCD12A. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.
## Trivia
- Treasure Dye counts as a [[Junk Sinker|Junk]] item while fishing with [[Junk Sinker]] in [[Backwater Bayou]], meaning effects like [[Attributes|Boot Fisher]] or [[Weather|Acid Rain]] also work on it.
## Gallery
- Treasure Dyed Leather Armor
- Treasure Bucket of Dye Treasure [[Bucket of Dye]]

# Treasure Hook
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Fishing, Tools
[Treasure Hook]
Type: Rod Part ([[Rod Parts]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Skill Level ([[Skills]]) Requirement: Fishing XXV (25) ([[Fishing]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TREASURE_HOOK
Tooltip: Only allows you to catch items and Treasure.; Talk to Roddy in the Backwater Bayou to apply this Hook to a Fishing Rod.; ❣ Requires Fishing Skill 25.; LEGENDARY ROD PART
A Treasure Hook is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Rod Parts|Rod Part]] that lets the player only catch items and treasure.
## Obtaining
A Treasure Hook can be purchased from [[Junker Joel]] for 1 [[Bayou Water Orb]], 4 [[Old Leather Boot]], 32 [[Busted Belt Buckle]] and 256 (4 stacks) [[Rusty Coin]] after reaching [[Fishing|Fishing XXV (25)]].
## Usage
Applying a Treasure Hook to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] at [[Roddy]] will make it only catch items and treasure. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXV (25)]] to apply and use.
## Trivia
- Treasure Hook nullifies the effects of [[Sea Creature Chance]], [[Double Hook Chance]] and [[Trophy Chance]], without changing the stats themselves.

The Trunk Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Trunk Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on May 2, 2026. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 4,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Trunk Skin can be applied to a [[Fig Armor|Fig Cap]] or [[Helix Armor|Helix Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 21 different versions.
- This skin is swappable between 21 wood-worked trunk variants!

# Ubik's Cube
Categories: Legendary items, Pages containing unknown information, Items
[Ubik's Cube]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ? Unknown (This information is unknown. If you know this information, please edit the page and replace this.)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: UBIKS_CUBE
Tooltip: Ability: Split or Steal RIGHT CLICK Click a Player or NPC to challenge them to a game of Split or Steal.; Cooldown: 2h; Ability: Stats LEFT CLICK View the Split or Steal stats of other players and NPCs.; LEGENDARY
Ubik's Cube is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
Ubik's Cube can be obtained from [[Ubik Von Neumann]] in [[The Mountaintop]] after completing the [[Ubik Von Neumann|Split or Steal]] minigame.
## Usage
Ubik's Cube can be used to play a round of [[Ubik Von Neumann|Split or Steal]] with any player or NPC. This has a cooldown of 2hr. Attempting to play a round of Split or Steal will display the following text:
SPLIT! You need to wait [time] before you can play again.
It also has a left-click ability, which allows players to view the [[Ubik Von Neumann|Split or Steal]] stats of any player or NPC.
## Trivia
- Ubik's Cube is a reference to the toy that the item is modeled after, the Rubik's Cube.
- The Split or Steal game is based on the Prisoner's Dilemma.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added Ubik's Cube.|
Linked from: [[Motes]], [[Ubik Von Neumann]]

# Ultimate Carrot Candy

[Shop]

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 532,940.8x (8,327 stacks plus 12.800000000047 (532,940.8 in total)) [[Carrot]] 1x [[Ultimate Carrot Candy Upgrade]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 1 [[Ultimate Carrot Candy Upgrade]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ULTIMATE_CARROT_CANDY
Tooltip: Consumable; Carrot Candy gives pets boosts of Pet EXP!; Pets can only eat 10 candy in their lifetime!; Grants 1,500,000 Pet EXP.; Right-click on your summoned pet to feed it!; LEGENDARY
Ultimate Carrot Candy is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that grants a [[Pets|Pet]] an instant [[Pets|XP]] boost of 1,500,000. Each pet can only use up to 10x [[Candy (disambiguation)|Carrot Candies]] of any level in their lifetime.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Ultimate Carrot Candy Upgrade]] 8x [[Superb Carrot Candy]]|Recipe: [[Superb Carrot Candy]] ×8 + [[Ultimate Carrot Candy Upgrade]] → Ultimate Carrot Candy x10|
## Usage
Ultimate Carrot Candies can be fed directly to a pet to give them 1,500,000 XP each.
Alternatively, a [[Caducous Feeder]] can consume up to [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]] 2 Ultimate Carrot Candy to gain a pet 1,650,000 XP per candy with a 20 hour cooldown. Ultimate Carrot Candy fed in this way don't count towards the limit, nor do they show up as candy used on the pet.
[[Ultimate Carrot Candy]] 2 Ultimate Carrot Candy can be given to [[Maxwell]] to unlock his [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]].
[[Ultimate Carrot Candy]] 1 Ultimate Carrot Candy can also be fed to [[Fann|Hootie]] to unlock the 3rd Training Slot at [[Fann]].

|Jan 16, 2024|0.19.10|Ultimate Carrot Candy now gives 1,500,000 XP instead of 1,000,000.|

The Undead Ender Dragon Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Undead Ender Dragon Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on July 22, 2022. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 7,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Undead Ender Dragon Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Ender Dragon Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

Unstable Dragon Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set that is focused on [[Crit Chance]] and [[Crit Damage]]. The full set can be crafted from 240 [[Dragon Fragment|Unstable Dragon Fragments]] and is one of the eight [[Dragon Armor]] sets. It has an ability where it occasionally strikes nearby mobs with lightning. This ability does not work on private islands.
You require at least [[Combat]] 16 (XVI) to be able to use this set and, if a dungeon item, [[Catacombs]] 10 (X) to be able to use this set while in [[Dungeons]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting

|Unstable Dragon Chestplate ([[Unstable Dragon Armor]])|None|80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Unstable Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×8 → [[Unstable Dragon Armor]]|
|Unstable Dragon Leggings ([[Unstable Dragon Armor]])|None|70x (1 stack plus 6 (70 in total)) Unstable Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×7 → [[Unstable Dragon Armor]]|
|Unstable Dragon Boots ([[Unstable Dragon Armor]])|None|40x Unstable Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])|Recipe: [[Dragon Fragment]] x10 ×4 → [[Unstable Dragon Armor]]|

## Tips
- This is a good cheap alternative to Shadow Assassin armor in Dungeons, as it gives similar EHP and gives a lot of Crit Chance and Crit Damage, which makes it complement the Flower of Truth quite nicely.
- Players tend to buy [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] as the next step up after this set. Though for those struggling to obtain the former, this is a decent set to hold on to.
- The full set bonus ability will not activate while riding pets such as the rock pet.
## Trivia
- The full set bonus used to be able to rack up kills with the [[Raider Sword]], usually on [[Tarantula Spider|Tarantula Spiders]] from [[Tarantula Minion|Tarantula Minions]]. This allowed mob grinders to gain more drops from the minions, due to [[Enchantments|Looting]], and gain coins in the purse since each kill with the Raider Sword would grant coins. This was removed by the full set bonus being disabled on private islands.
- The armor set costs , making it a good choice for a starter armor.

Tooltip: Gear Score: 505 (550); Damage: +270 (+297); Strength: +145 (+159.5); Ferocity: +60 (+60); Intelligence: +60 (+66); Gemstones: [] [⚔]; Deals +50% damage to  Wither mobs.; Grants +1  Damage and +1  Strength per Catacombs level.; Right-click to use your class ability!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor VII Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON SWORD
The Valkyrie is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked upon completion of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
The Valkyrie is one of the four [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] variants, alongside the [[Hyperion]], [[Astraea]], and [[Scylla]], focusing on [[Strength]], [[Ferocity]] and [[Berserk]] builds.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|8 [[Diamante's Handle]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] [[Hyperion]] [[Astraea]]|Recipe: [[Diamante's Handle]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Scylla]]/[[Hyperion]]/[[Astraea]] → Valkyrie|
## Usage
Sword:
Valkyrie [[Damage]]: +270 [[Strength]]: +145 [[Ferocity]]: +60 [[Intelligence]]: +60
[Effects]
[Deals +50% damage to Wither mobs.; Grants +1 Damage and +1 Strength per Catacombs level.; Right-click to use your class ability!]
### Crafting
The Valkyrie can be crafted into another [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] variant using the corresponding recipe, keeping all enchantments and upgrades. Items used in the crafting recipe are consumed.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hyperion]]|None|8x [[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] [[Astraea]] [[Valkyrie]] Valkyrie|Recipe: [[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Scylla]]/[[Astraea]]/Valkyrie → [[Hyperion]]|
|[[Astraea]]|None|8 [[Jolly Pink Rock]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] [[Hyperion]] [[Valkyrie]] Valkyrie|Recipe: [[Jolly Pink Rock]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Scylla]]/[[Hyperion]]/Valkyrie → [[Astraea]]|
|[[Scylla]]|None|8 [[Bigfoot's Bola]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Hyperion]] [[Astraea]] [[Valkyrie]] Valkyrie|Recipe: [[Bigfoot's Bola]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Hyperion]]/[[Astraea]]/Valkyrie → [[Scylla]]|
It can have [[Necron's Blade Scrolls]] applied to it, granting their effects on right click.
## Trivia
- The Valkyrie references female figures of Old Norse mythology, who carried warriors who died in battle to Valhalla, the hall of Odin.
  - The Valkyrie is the only [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] variant to reference Norse mythology: the [[Hyperion]] references the Greek titan, the [[Astraea]] references the virgin goddess of justice and purity, and the [[Scylla]] references the sea monster found opposite Charbydis.
- The Valkyrie has the highest base [[Ferocity]] of any item, at 60 [[Ferocity]].
- Donating the Valkyrie to the [[Museum]] grants credit for all [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] varients except the [[Hyperion]].

The Vampire Dentist Relic is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is unlocked at [[Vampire Slayer|Vampire Slayer V (5)]] and is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Vampire Slayer V (5) ([[Vampire Slayer]])|1x [[Unfanged Vampire Part]] 8x [[Bloodbadge]]|Recipe: [[Bloodbadge]] ×8 + [[Unfanged Vampire Part]] → Vampire Dentist Relic|
## Usage
Accessory:
Vampire Dentist Relic [[Rift Time]]: +30 [[Hearts]]: +1
[Effects]
[Heal +1 Hearts from healing melons.]
Accessory:
Vampire Dentist Relic (Outside) ([[Vampire Dentist Relic]]) [[Health Regen]]: +5 [[Vitality]]: +1
[Effects]
[Applies a natural regen tick when you deal melee damage. (20s cooldown)]
The Vampire Dentist Relic's ability in the [[Rift Dimension]] affects the [[Healing Melon]] and its upgrades.
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], the Vampire Dentist Relic can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the [[Inverted Sirius|Rift Transfer Chest]] next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Once outside the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]], the Vampire Dentist Relic grants +5 [[Health Regen]] and +1 [[Vitality]]. Additionally, dealing melee damage applies a natural regen tick with a 20 second cooldown.

[Upgrades]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VERMIN_PEST_TRAP
Tooltip: Place in a Garden plot and fill with bait to catch  Pests while offline.; Maximum Capacity: 4 Pests Limit of 3 Traps; Field Mice are 4x more likely to be caught in this type of trap!; LEGENDARY
A Vermin Trap is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
Vermin Traps can be purchased from [[Pesthunter Phillip]] for 200 [[Pests (Currency)|Pests]], 1 [[Mouse Trap]], and 1 [[Fire in a Bottle]].
## Usage
Vermin Traps can be placed in [[The Garden|Garden Plots]] to occasionally catch [[Pests]]. Up to 3 Vermin Traps can be placed at once. Pest baits can be placed inside to increase the chance of certain [[Pests]] spawning. Every [[Pests|Pest]] that spawns in a trap consumes one Pest Bait.
- [[Compost]] attracts [[Mosquito|Mosquitos]] and [[Earthworm|Earthworms]].
- [[Plant Matter]] attracts [[Locust|Locusts]] and [[Slug|Slugs]].
- [[Dung]] attracts [[Beetle|Beetles]] and [[Fly|Flies]].
- [[Honey Jar|Honey Jars]] attract [[Moth|Moths]] and [[Cricket|Crickets]].
- [[Tasty Cheese]] attracts [[Rat|Rats]] and [[Mite|Mites]].
- [[Jelly]] attracts [[Dragonfly|Dragonflies]] (only during the day), [[Firefly|Fireflies]] (only during the night), and [[Praying Mantis|Praying Mantises]].
- [[Moondew]] guarantees catching [[Lunar Moth|Lunar Moths]].
Additionally, [[Field Mouse|Field Mice]] are 4x more likely to be caught.

The Wand of Atonement is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Wands|Healing Wand]] and [[Support Items|Support Item]] unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VII (7)]]. It is an upgraded version of the [[Wand of Restoration]] and the final upgrade in the [[Wands|Healing Wand]] line.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

## Usage
Wand:
Wand of Atonement
[Effects]
[Ability: Huge Heal RIGHT CLICK; Heal 120 Health per second for 6s.; Wand heals don't stack.; Vitality Cost (Vitality): 25; Cooldown: 1s]
## Tips
- The set bonuses of [[Revenant Armor]] and [[Reaper Armor]] increase healing from [[Wands|Healing Wands]] by 50%.
- Each armor piece enchanted with [[Habanero Tactics|Habanero Tactics V]] grants 7.5% additional healing from [[Wands|Healing Wands]].
- Use the /reclaim command in order to reclaim previous upgrades that no longer serve a purpose to this item
## Trivia
- Although consecutive casts do not stack, the first healing tick is applied immediately. With enough [[Vitality]], repeatedly casting the ability could heal 240 [[Health]] per second.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WARDEN_HELMET
Tooltip: Health: +300; Defense: +100; Ability: Brute Force Halves your  Speed but grants +20% base weapon damage for every 25  Speed.; This item can be reforged!; ☠ Requires Zombie Slayer 8.; LEGENDARY HELMET
A Warden Helmet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VIII (8)]]. It sacrifices its user's [[Speed]] for [[Damage]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Zombie Slayer VIII (8) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Warden Heart]] 2x [[Revived Heart]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Iron Block]]|Recipe: [[Revived Heart]] ×2 + [[Warden Heart]] + [[Enchanted Iron Block]] x64 ×2 → Warden Helmet|
## Usage
Helmet:
Warden Helmet [[Health]]: +300 [[Defense]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Brute Force; Halves your Speed but grants +20% base weapon damage for every 25 Speed.]
A Warden Helmet requires [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VIII (8)]] to use.
When worn, the Warden Helmet halves its user's [[Speed]]. For every 25 [[Speed]] remaining, the helmet grants an additive +20% [[Damage]] multiplier, and an additive +1% [[Damage]] multiplier on top of that. This affects all damage, not just the damage applied to weapons (i.e. fists would be affected). The [[Speed]] cap is applied before halving, meaning the maximum cap with a 400 [[Speed]] cap is 200 [[Speed]], which grants a +161% [[Damage]] multiplier. Items that increase the [[Speed]] cap, such as a [[Black Cat Pet]] or [[Unalloyed Speed]], can increase the maximum [[Damage]] multiplier.
The maximum possible [[Damage]] multiplier in realistic scenarios is +241% [[Damage]] with the following:
- [[Black Cat Pet]] with [[Unalloyed Speed]]
- Being in the [[Catacombs]] with [[Attributes|Catacombs Cap X]]
However this is generally worse for damage than using a [[Golden Dragon Pet]], especially when paired with [[Hephaestus Remedies]].
While not useful in practice, it is possible to get a +261% damage multiplier if the player additionally holds a [[Cactus Knife]] while on the [[The Garden]].
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Spartan Warden Helmet Skin]]|[[Spartan Warden Helmet Skin]]|ULTIMATE ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Summer 2026 - 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 320 Spartan variants.; ▶ Keeps track of the distance walked while wearing this skin.|
|[[Spartan Warden Helmet Skin]]|[[Spartan Warden Helmet Skin]]|ULTIMATE ([[Rarity]])|(same as above)||
|[[Sentinel Warden Skin]]|[[Sentinel Warden Skin]]|ULTIMATE ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - December 23, 2023 - 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 8 variants, unlocked from [[Revenant Horror]] Slayer XP!; ▶ Modifies the look of your [[Revenant Horror]] Boss!|
|[[Sentinel Warden Skin]]|[[Sentinel Warden Skin]]|ULTIMATE ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - December 23, 2023 - 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
|[[True Warden Skin]]|[[True Warden Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - May 27, 2022 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin is animated!|
|[[True Warden Skin]]|[[True Warden Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - May 27, 2022 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Trivia
- The Warden Helmet's Brute Force doesn't round strictly to multiples of 25 [[Speed]]. A player with 176 [[Speed]] will deal more damage than the same player with 175 [[Speed]].
- In the [[Catacombs]], several statues feature helmets with the Warden Helmet's texture.
- The Warden Helmet was first leaked by former 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon in a Discord message.
  - This version had 375 [[Health]] and 150 [[Defense]], and increased damage by 25% for every 50 [[Speed]] above 100.
  - Several of the final helmet in its respective armor set's line are either recolored versions or variations of the Warden Helmet: this includes the [[Crimson Armor|Crimson Helmet]], [[Fermento Armor|Fermento]] and [[Helianthus Armor|Helianthus Helmet]], [[Clover Helmet]], and [[Armor of Divan|Helmet of Divan]], among others.

Tooltip: Wart Eater I Grants a +0.01% chance to obtain a Warty when breaking Nether Warts.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 20.; LEGENDARY FOREST SHARD (ID L13)
The Wartybug Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Wart Eater]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Wartybug Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Dragonfly Shard]]
- Second: [[Lunar Moth Shard]]
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Wartybug Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Wart Eater]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Wart Eater ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +0.01%–0.1% chance to obtain a [[Warty]] when breaking Nether Warts.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Wartybug Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Wartybug Shard has the following description:
- These insects feed on the sap of warts, their specialized mouthparts designed to pierce and extract the nutritious liquid.

# Wild Strawberry Dye
Aliases: Strawberry Dye, Strawberry dye, Wild Strawberry Dye (Old)

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_WILD_STRAWBERRY
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #FF43A4!; LEGENDARY DYE
Wild Strawberry Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
The player has a 0.000000006667% (1 in 150,000,000) chance of obtaining a Wild Strawberry Dye from breaking any mature crop, affected by [[Overbloom]].
[[Vincent]] also has a 0.04% (1 in 2,500) chance of rewarding a Wild Strawberry Dye as a Garden Visitor.
## Usage
Wild Strawberry Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece that has base texture of [[Leather Armor]] by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #FF43A4.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

A Will-o'-wisp is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Deployables|Deployable]].
## Obtaining
### Forge
A Will-o'-wisp can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain X (10)]]. It takes 24 hours to forge.

  - 8 [[Enchanted Emerald Block]] 1,280 [[Enchanted Emerald]] 204,800 (3,200 stacks) [[Emerald]]
## Usage
Using the ability of a Will-o'-wisp costs 50% of player's max [[Mana]], this can be reduced down to 40% using [[Mana Disintegrator|Mana Disintegrators]]. Casting the ability grants up to 10 players in 30 block radius (or all players in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]]) the following stats:
- +100 [[Mining Speed]]
- +25 [[Mining Fortune]]
- +20 [[Heat Resistance]]
- +10 [[Cold Resistance]]
- +2.5 [[Gemstone Spread]]
When used alongside multiple or different lantern deployables, only one deployable effect applies and only the one of higher rarity will apply its effects.
## Trivia
- In folklore, Will-o'-wisp or also Will-o'-the-wisp is a mysterious light, sometimes an entity — either a benevolent or a malicious one — that guides travelers, especially in forests or swamps.
